# Repair report: PeriodsAndSpecialValues

The two reviews use the same finding IDs. The first group below is review a (F01–F18); the second is review b (F01–F13). Overlaps are resolved once and cross-referenced explicitly. These repairs and finite mathematical checks are not an independent acceptance review.

# Review a

## F01 (P3) — applied

Forbidden option and PR/head references → no options, no unused Tau Ceti import, and supplier names with verified sections. The same cleanup covers all four stale head citations and the source-register links.

## F02 (P1) — applied differently

The absence claim is partly false: Completed/NumberFieldArithmetic/README.md:8 exists; PolylogarithmFunctions, K3SurfacesAndSymplecticBoundedness and ComputationalNumberTheory precede this package at merge positions 36, 38 and 120. Five alleged suppliers are absent. Their uses → NE §7.9, general local Borel and semistable targets, conditional arithmetic-witness transport and the CM-regulator gap.

## F03 (P1) — not actionable in this package: supplier extensions require their owners

Registered the modular-motive, motivic-cohomology/Adams and framed-relative-motive gaps in the README and the plan’s top-level gaps list. They require supplier edits outside this package. The arbitrary-pair comparison and virtual determinant need no gaps: their owners already state them (review b F03 and F05).

## F04 (P1) — applied

Reverse supply claims → one-way imports. MC supplies evaluated periods, AL supplies local analytic data, and NC supplies generic groupoids; this package owns their special-value comparisons. The phantom MotivicEtaleKTheory line → the earlier NE §7.9 object with its inverse BF comparison. Downstream claims no longer create reverse edges.

## F05 (P3) — applied

Quadrature boxes, outward rounding, error budgets and recovery procedures → real integral transformations, analytic midpoint bounds, a quotient-stability inequality and exact covariance. Removed the computational supplier contract, Johansson citation, and enclosure promises throughout Summary, Scope, PS.7 and worked examples.

## F06 (P2) — applied differently

Added mzv_allTwo and the arbitrary-prime determinant criterion. Brown (3.7), B line 416, gives the former; B line 885 states the latter for every prime. The two-adic specialization stays in the README, avoiding a duplicate Lean theorem. Empty-index and empty-matrix Checks give 1=1 and det=1.

## F07 (P2) — applied

Whole rational tangent space → H¹_f(ℚ_p,V_p); the lift to integral local points is restricted to a small open neighborhood. Added restriction-independence of its Haar measure, and corrected the API and supplier contract. BK line 1252 states the local domain; BK line 1255 gives the rational exponential.

## F08 (P2) — applied

Unqualified local complex → RΓ_f, with its finite/singular triangle and explicitly typed determinant factor. BF line 895 defines this finite complex. Added ℚ_p(1) over ℚ_ℓ: finite cohomology is zero, but full H¹ and H² each have dimension one.

## F09 (P2) — applied

Weight ≤−3 only → local points in weight ≤−1, convergent measure in weight ≤−3, and regularized measure in weight ≤−1 under the stated continuation. Added independence of S, form and local neighborhood, and elliptic compatibility. BK line 1236 gives the point domain; BK line 1283 gives regularization.

## F10 (P2) — applied

Odd-prime analytic sums → every prime in both files. Removed both hp binders and the geometric PS.8.14 prerequisite from this analytic proof. At p=2, B₂=1 and the valuation slope is 1/4; harmonic loss remains logarithmic. Geometric Frobenius hypotheses remain separate.

## F11 (P3) — applied

TensorWords locator → Coaugmented/Basic.lean, lines 84 and 228. Gram-coordinate locator → StandardCoordinates.lean, line 53, all relative to the pinned Tau Ceti tree. Replaced the remaining “current” wording with “at the pin”.

## F12 (P3) — applied

Self-Ext equals group cohomology → the regular-local-ring obstruction. For ℤ₂[C₂], dimension is 1 and embedding dimension is 2, so residue-field projective dimension is infinite (Stacks line 12552). Self-Ext¹ has dimension 2; ordinary H¹(C₂,𝔽₂) has dimension 1. Nonperfectness is retained.

## F13 (P3) — applied differently

Kept scalar and boundary witnesses in README Checks, including residue values, harmonic corrections, matrices, pair weights, index controls, unit algebraicity and inverse Tate powers. Added no mirror declarations or per-definition examples. Removed the old scalar-definition/example catalogue from Lean; retained only definitions required by headline theorems.

## F14 (P3) — applied differently

Undocumented or catalogue declarations → removed unless needed by a headline. Every retained declaration has a mathematical docstring. The retained mzvDecodeAux is described in PS.9.2, including discarded trailing zeros and the valid-word restriction. README coverage was checked for all 43 retained names.

## F15 (P3) — applied

Absent register → sources.toml with one key per work, legal-source provenance, versions, locators and targets. It records 28 certified works, uncertified Burns–Greither, and the settled Tate-legal waiver. BV-final is reused for both versions. All initial fetch failures were resolved legally; no missing-text request remains.

## F16 (P3) — applied

Dimension-zero finite-zeta label → completed Dedekind zeta, with finite factor named separately. Translated fixed-point label → p=1±i/√2, equivalently p−1=±i/√2. Recomputed Γ_R(2)ζ(2)=π/6 and both reflection fixed-point equations.

## F17 (P1) — applied

Criticality/sign-period cycle → explicit sign-isomorphism hypotheses in PS.0.4 and their criticality corollary in PS.1.3. Computed real Tate cochains before the integral determinant in PS.4.1; PS.4.4 no longer needs PS.4.1. Defined predicted order in PS.3.3 from the two reduced ranks; PS.5.2 consumes it.

## F18 (P3) — applied

Truncated relative-cohomology numbering → Propositions 3.2.9 and 3.2.13 and Lemma 3.2.14. Exact evidence: HMS-book line 3527, HMS-book line 3541 and HMS-book line 3566. The corresponding comparison is imported from MC §5.7, not reconstructed here.


# Review b

## F01 (P1) — applied differently

Extends review a F02. Removed the two later arithmetic supplier contracts and the three purported imported rank theorems. PS.6.7 now transports an explicit BBKRankZeroOne witness through actual Selmer base change. It claims no L-value-to-definite-period implication and no rank theorem missing the later roadmap’s uniform span premises.

## F02 (P1) — applied

Nonexistent Selmer §§4.3, 4.5, 4.6 → FiniteFlatGroupsAndIntegralPadicHodgeTheory §§R07.P3, R07.P5. Retained Selmer §§4.7, 4.9, 4.24, 4.25 with their actual subjects. Updated the exact contract, PS.6.1–PS.6.2 and their prerequisites; regulator comparisons remain explicit triple data.

## F03 (P2) — applied

Arbitrary-pair input gap and duplicate evaluation construction → imports of MC §§5.7, 6.7–6.9. PS.2 compares the supplied β-point with I=β⁻¹. Corrected the accompanying product-sign pattern: swapping forms and cycles contributes two signs, hence paired value +1. HMS-book line 3541 supplies Künneth; MC §6.7 states both-sign cancellation.

## F04 (P1) — applied

AL.5 critical-value theorem attribution → ModularSymbolsPadicLFunctions §§1.1–1.2, with its signed periods, Gauss sum, factorial and Tate factors. AL.5 supplies Euler corrections and leading terms. The modular-motive realization remains an explicit MC gap; no claim that L/Ω alone is algebraic was added.

## F05 (P2) — applied

Shared GAK virtual-object gap and local reconstruction → NE §2.7’s determinant category and functor. Removed the duplicate virtualPerfectDeterminant interface. Retained GAK §§4.15–4.17 for relative groups. The commutative determinant-line functor is not asserted to identify the whole virtual category with graded lines.

## F06 (P2) — applied

Arbitrary abelian extension over totally real k → totally real abelian F/k and finite S containing infinity, p and ramification in the §11.7 contract. PS.5.4 uses the maximal totally real field and §11.1’s CM twist before descent. The abelian Tate conclusion retains its original ℤ[1/2][G] scope.

## F07 (P2) — applied

“Compact support without p” → compact support for S containing all archimedean and p-adic places. Corrected the supplier contract and both real-dyadic references. The complete Tate-cochain calculation remains available; no nonexistent p-omitted compact-support model is used.

## F08 (P2) — not actionable in this package: K3 and lattice extensions require their owners

Replaced fictitious K3.0 nodes by the actual §§3.3 and 4.2 statements, without stale declaration names. Registered gap:k3-betti-even-unimodular. The checked completed IntegralLattices roadmap also excludes classification; registered its Part II primitive-embedding/classification gap. Huybrechts line 525 gives the geometric theorem. This package cannot add either supplier theorem.

## F09 (P2) — applied

R01 labels and nonexistent conductor node → AGR §§2.4–2.6 and §3.3, with the actual conductor declarations. Frobenius semisimplification is SR.5.2. A compatible automorphic realization and AL.4 supply local-parameter comparison; no Layer 3 local–global theorem is attributed to AGR.

## F10 (P3) — applied

Corrected all eight contract patterns: duality §§0.5–0.6; Tate n-gons §3.6; PolylogarithmFunctions; crystalline cup products CR.3; MC Layers 3–6; rational interpolation NE.7.14; the two actual AG2.6 headings; and elliptic regulator basis independence. The index-squared regulator law stays this package’s comparison target.

## F11 (P3) — applied

NumberFieldArithmetic → its Completed link. IntegralHeckeAndGaloisDeterminants → the earlier roadmap’s name and §0.6 without the misleading main-tree prefix. Corrected every occurrence, including determinant and reduced-norm uses in PS.4–PS.5.

## F12 (P3) — applied

Unused Tate import → deleted. The final file imports only Mathlib. The README still locates the pinned Tate Hodge declarations correctly, but comments about them no longer force an unused module import or a targeted build.

## F13 (P3) — applied differently

Deleted all eight one-tactic sorried declarations and their unused scalar infrastructure; retained their mathematical facts and boundary values in the README. They were not proved in place or converted into example padding. The same headline-only cleanup removed other controls, scalar identities and the 38-example catalogue.

# Required checks and gate failures

- Elaboration: `lake env lean` on `packages/PeriodsAndSpecialValues/Suggested.lean`, from the pinned content checkout, exited 0. Its only diagnostics were 17 expected admitted-theorem warnings. No TauCeti module build was needed after deleting the unused import.
- Mechanical signature audit: one scratch module contained the final declarations and `#check @name` for each of the 43 retained declarations. Elaboration exited 0. I compared every displayed type with its README statement: the Legendre inequalities retain real parameter and positive-panel hypotheses, the Dwork sums retain the prime instance and include 2, and the determinant criterion retains the prime instance, nonzero diagonal and zero-entry alternatives. No needed section binder disappeared. Final changes after that audit were docstrings only.
- Dependency check, with the supplied merge order and `--movable`, exited 0 with this exact output:

```text
depcheck: 14 roadmaps, 128 edges, 0 problem(s)
```

There was no UPWARD, CYCLE or moved line.

- Source-manifest validation exited 0 with 0 problems. All 30 entries have the required bibliographic fields, locator scheme, legal-access class and status. The initial missing-text list is empty after successful legal retries. The BG DOI was verified against the [institutional publication record](https://kclpure.kcl.ac.uk/portal/en/publications/on-the-equivariant-tamagawa-number-conjecture-for-tate-motives/) and corrected to 10.1007/s00222-003-0291-x; its status remains uncertified. BV-final records DOI 10.4171/DMS/4/5 together with its arXiv locator, as verified in the [publisher’s record](https://ems.press/books/dms/254/5032).
- Citation resolution lists no unresolved roadmap citations and no unresolved Lean names. That tool reported that it could not inspect pinned Lake packages, so pinned declarations were checked directly in the provided trees instead of inferring their existence from that output.
- `git diff --check` exited 0. All edited and added files are within the allowed package paths; no machine path appears in the deliverables.
- The package-content gate was run against an isolated read-through of this package in the gate’s expected roadmap layout. Every content-rule count is zero. Its sole remaining full-gate count is `dirty=1`, reporting the untracked read-through directory in that scratch Git tree. That is not a package-content finding; no commit was made to hide it. The worker must run its normal checkout/commit stage. Running the generic gate directly on the board layout instead reports its expected missing TauCetiRoadmap paths, so that layout invocation is not a successful package check.

The listed submission failures were repaired as follows:

The final acceptance file has 26 real-bodied definitions, 17 admitted headline theorems, no examples, no admitted data, no option overrides, one Mathlib import and no TauCeti imports. Its 259 lines are below the limit. The new declarations are mzv_allTwo and det_ne_zero_of_padic_triangular. The latter subsumes the README’s two-adic application.

# Ownership and consequences

The main checkout and all supplied sibling heads were searched by mathematical object. The roadmap service additionally searched the indexed open roadmap heads and main. No helper agents were used. Number-field regulators are owned locally by PS.3.2a because no BorelRegulators supplier exists: this includes the general stable arithmetic comparison, primitive K-theory comparison, regulator isomorphism, Adams weights and factor-two normalization. ArithmeticLocallySymmetricSpaces supplies the geometric quotient and relative-cochain carriers, not that stable theorem. The general equivariant Dwork semistable-model theorem moves into PS.8.17 because its alleged supplier is absent and the available downstream Dwork work cannot be an input. It is stated for every N>1 with residue characteristic prime to N before the N=5 adapter.

Six exact supplier gaps are recorded with identical slugs in `packages/PeriodsAndSpecialValues/README.md` and the top-level gaps list of `packages/PeriodsAndSpecialValues/design/plan.json`: modular-eigenprojector-motive, motivic-cohomology-adams, framed-relative-motives, k3-betti-even-unimodular, cm-elliptic-regulator, and indefinite-lattice-classification. The plan did not exist; its only top-level key is gaps. Supplier edits, the IntegralLattices Part II and the precise CM arithmetic formula require their owners. These gaps have not been represented as available theorems. In particular the CM requirement is not settled by a rescaled arbitrary regulator matrix.

No later SelmerIwasawaCohomologyPartII or GanGrossPrasadUnitaryGroups theorem is imported. PS.6.7 is conditional transport; the original advertised arithmetic rank endpoints are not proved by this package. PS.6.4 likewise requires its exact scalar BSD witness, including exceptional-prime comparisons. There is no merge-order edit and no unconditional new BSD claim.

Exact-name inspection found no references to the fully qualified package namespace or deleted declarations on main or the indexed open roadmap heads. The substring search returned only EffectiveDiophantineMethods’ different arctanPartialSum and arctanPartialSum_error names; neither cites the deleted arctanPartial declaration. The generic names of discarded scalar controls remain mathematical facts in prose, not claimed declarations. Consumers should use the general prime-adic criterion rather than require a second named two-adic signature. The arbitrary-pair comparison, marked evaluation and virtual determinant category remain with their verified earlier owners.

# Adversarial mathematics pass

Finite tests are witnesses for conventions and hypotheses, not proofs of the planned deep theorems. For unavailable geometric carriers the rows compute their stated finite realization models and numerical consequences; they do not assert the missing construction exists. Each main target, each interface clause, each Check and each retained Lean declaration has its own row below. Clauses in one target share the displayed finite model when they concern the same construction. Supplier-gap rows remain conditional. All modified signs, domains and normalizations were tested against the stated alternative, including the two product signs in PS.2 and the regularization factor in PS.6.2.

An independent rational-arithmetic script recomputed 46 groups: all five quartic isometries and their ordered product; nilpotence; coefficients through degree five in the hypergeometric identity and degree four in the mirror inverse; 45 quintic pairing instances; Dwork recurrences and valuation bounds for p=2,3,5,7 and n=1,…,128; strict harmonic weights; the Zagier matrix; general-prime determinant examples including size zero; word products and Hoffman counts through weight 12. Real Simpson evaluations were used only as additional numerical checks of the Legendre inequality, not as integral proofs. Minimum sampled valuation-bound margins were 1.75, 1.7777777777, 1.84 and 1.8775510204 respectively.

| def stuffles | empty words; 01 shuffle 01; [2] stuffle [2]; invalid trailing zero | shuffle multiplicities=4,2; stuffle=[2,2],[2,2],[4]; encode [2,1]=011, decode 001101=[3,1,2]; decode 0=[] while encode [0]=encode [1]=1 | finite model agrees | real body |
| theorem mzv_stuffle | weight-four shuffle and stuffle | ζ(2)²/ζ(4)=5/2; RHS coefficients give ζ(3,1)/ζ(4)=1/4 and ζ(2,2)/ζ(4)=3/4; π⁴ coefficients=1/360,1/120 | finite model agrees | headline with explicit elaborated binders |
| theorem mzv_shuffle | weight-four shuffle and stuffle | ζ(2)²/ζ(4)=5/2; RHS coefficients give ζ(3,1)/ζ(4)=1/4 and ζ(2,2)/ζ(4)=3/4; π⁴ coefficients=1/360,1/120 | finite model agrees | headline with explicit elaborated binders |
| theorem mzv_two_one | regularized words [1,1],[1,2]; product 1 with 01 | ρ((T²−ζ2)/2)=T²/2; discrepancy at [1,2]=ζ(2,1)−ζ3=0; shuffle minus stuffle=011−001 | finite model agrees | headline with explicit elaborated binders |
| def zagierCoeff | (a,b,r)=(0,0,1),(1,0,1),(1,0,2),(0,1,1),(0,1,2) | coefficients=1,−2,9/2,3,−11/2; denominators=1,1,2,1,2 | finite model agrees | real body |
| def oneThree | a,b=(0,0),(1,0),(0,1); all-two m=0,1,2 | indices=[3],[2,3],[3,2]; coefficients=1,(−2,9/2),(3,−11/2); sum of weight-five formulas=ζ2ζ3−ζ5; all-two π-normalized factors=1,1/6,1/120 | finite model agrees | real body |
| theorem zagier_evaluation | a,b=(0,0),(1,0),(0,1); all-two m=0,1,2 | indices=[3],[2,3],[3,2]; coefficients=1,(−2,9/2),(3,−11/2); sum of weight-five formulas=ζ2ζ3−ζ5; all-two π-normalized factors=1,1/6,1/120 | finite model agrees | headline with explicit elaborated binders |
| theorem det_ne_zero_of_padic_triangular | n=0; p=2,3; singular controls | det empty=1; [[1,1],[2,1]] det=−1; [[1,1],[3,1]] det=−2; Hoffman det=5/2 with v₂=−1; all-ones singular det=0 fails below-diagonal hypothesis | finite model agrees | headline with explicit elaborated binders |
| def IsHoffman | weights 0,5,6, with levels 0,1,2 | B₀₀={()}, B₅₁={[2,3],[3,2]}, B₆₀={[2,2,2]}, B₆₂={[3,3]}, B₆₁=∅; counts=1,2,1,1,0 | finite model agrees | real body |
| def hoffmanDim | weights 0,…,8 | d=1,0,1,1,1,2,2,3,4; (1−t²−t³)Σd_nt^n has coefficients 1,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0 | finite model agrees | real body |
